📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期麻疹病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 提供者对风疹 IgG 筛查的依从性从干预前的 63.3% 增加到干预后的 75.1%,增加了 11.8%。
  • CRISPR-Cas12a 检测检测到每个反应低至 10 个拷贝的 MeV 模板,Probit 回归估计的 LoD95 为每个反应 32.25 个拷贝,优于单独的 RT-LAMP(每个反应 97.32 个拷贝)。该检测显示出很高的分析特异性,与其他常见呼吸道病毒没有交叉反应,并且在 45-50 分钟内完成。
  • CD 光谱表明,除了少数例外,突变 降低了融合后核心稳定性,尽管它们与增强的融合活性有关。结构分析揭示了 HR2 内的空间堆积、氢键网络和螺旋稳定相互作用的局部扰动,而整体 6HB 结构保持保守。
  • XGBoost 的 AUC-ROC 为 0.838(准确度 81.3%;F1 0.840;MCC 0.624),社会人口因素对 暴发 预测的贡献最大(41.37%),其次是气候(39.22%)和医疗保健相关变量(19.41%)。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The overall societal cost of the outbreak was estimated at $5.4 million, with $53,522 per case and $1,817 per contact.
  • Provider adherence to Rubeola IgG screening increased from 63.3% pre-intervention to 75.1% post-intervention, representing an 11.8% increase.
  • The CRISPR-Cas12a assay detected MeV templates down to 10 copies per reaction, with a Probit regression-estimated LoD95 of 32.25 copies per reaction, outperforming RT-LAMP alone (97.32 copies per reaction).
  • CD spectroscopy showed that, with few exceptions, the mutations decreased postfusion core stability despite their association with enhanced fusion activity. [10.1021/acs.biochem.6c00182].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: BMC Infectious Diseases  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-04  |  DOI: 10.1186/s12879-026-14067-y  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

使用地理空间人工智能在孟加拉国进行区级 麻疹 暴发 预测

District-level measles outbreak prediction in Bangladesh using geospatial artificial intelligence
作者： Md. Abu Bokkor Shiddik, Md. Siddikur Rahman
摘要中文翻译尽管存在安全有效的 疫苗,但 麻疹 已重新成为日益严重的公共卫生威胁。在孟加拉国,反复出现的 暴发 和不断扩大的免疫差距凸显了主动进行地区级风险评估的迫切需要。本研究开发了一个可解释的机器学习框架,用于识别孟加拉国地区级 麻疹 暴发 风险的驱动因素并进行预测。区级 麻疹 监测 数据(2017-2023 年)与气候、社会人口、医疗保健能力和 疫苗接种 指标相结合。使用时间训练/测试分割以及在训练折叠中选择的 F1 优化决策阈值进行嵌套交叉验证,对八个机器学习分类器(决策树、支持 媒介 机器、朴素贝叶斯、随机森林、LightGBM、CatBoost、XGBoost 和 Elastic Net GLM)进行比较。XGBoost 的选择基于卓越的 AUC-ROC 和马修斯相关系数。SHapley Additive exPlanations 量化了 麻疹 暴发 预测的预测变量贡献。该模型在 2017-2023 年完整数据集上进行了重新训练,以预测 2024-2028 年地区级 暴发 概率,置信区间为 95%。麻疹 显示出明显的时空异质性,从 2017-2018 年的东北地区向 2021-2023 年的达卡周边地区转移。XGBoost 的 AUC-ROC 为 0.838(准确度 81.3%;F1 0.840;MCC 0.624),支持基于风险的地区优先级排序,而不是精确的病例级别预测。社会人口因素对 暴发 预测的贡献最大(41.37%),其次是气候(39.22%)和医疗保健相关变量(19.41%)。疫苗接种 覆盖范围显示出较低的独立重要性,反映了背景风险调节。最低气温、家庭规模和贫困人数是 麻疹 暴发 预测的主要预测因子。锡尔赫特、库什蒂亚和达卡是 麻疹 暴发 最脆弱的地区,而库尔纳和吉大港地区到 2028 年风险持续升高。孟加拉国的 麻疹 暴发 风险是由气候和社会经济脆弱性以及 疫苗接种 差距决定的。可解释的机器学习可以支持精准免疫和有针对性的 监测,同时需要对预测不确定性进行谨慎解释。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管存在安全有效的 麻疹,但 麻疹 已重新成为日益严重的公共卫生威胁,在孟加拉国,麻疹 的反复出现和不断扩大的免疫差距凸显了主动进行区级风险评估的迫切需要。
设计与对象
本研究开发了一个可解释的机器学习框架,使用 2017 年至 2023 年地区级 麻疹 监测 数据来识别孟加拉国地区级 麻疹 暴发 风险的驱动因素并预测。
核心方法
使用时间训练/测试分割和嵌套交叉验证与 F1 优化的决策阈值对八个机器学习分类器进行了比较,并且 SHapley Additive exPlanations 量化了预测器对 麻疹 暴发 预测的贡献。
主要结果
XGBoost 的 AUC-ROC 为 0.838(准确度 81.3%;F1 0.840;MCC 0.624),社会人口因素对 暴发 预测的贡献最大(41.37%),其次是气候(39.22%)和医疗保健相关变量(19.41%)。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为证明孟加拉国的 麻疹 暴发 风险是由气候和社会经济脆弱性以及 疫苗接种 差距造成的,他们强调了使用可解释的机器学习来支持精准免疫和有针对性的 监测 的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为有针对性的 疫苗接种 活动、精准免疫策略和地区级风险优先排序提供信息,以减轻孟加拉国的 麻疹 暴发 影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认,该模型支持基于风险的地区优先顺序,而不是精确的病例级别预测,并且需要对预测不确定性进行谨慎解释,这表明证据强度适中。

District-level measles outbreak prediction in Bangladesh using geospatial artificial intelligence

Authors: Md. Abu Bokkor Shiddik, Md. Siddikur Rahman
Original abstract (English)
Measles has re-emerged as a growing public health threat despite the availability of a safe and effective vaccine. In Bangladesh, recurring outbreaks and widening immunization disparities underscore the urgent need for proactive district-level risk assessment. This study developed an explainable machine learning framework to identify drivers of and forecast district-level measles outbreak risk in Bangladesh. District-level measles surveillance data (2017–2023) were combined with climatic, socio-demographic, healthcare capacity, and vaccination indicators. Eight machine learning classifiers (Decision Tree, Support Vector Machine, Naive Bayes, Random Forest, LightGBM, CatBoost, XGBoost, and an Elastic Net GLM) were compared using temporal train /test splitting, and nested cross-validation with F1-optimised decision thresholds selected within training fold. XGBoost was selected based on superior AUC-ROC and Matthews Correlation Coefficient. SHapley Additive exPlanations quantified predictor contributions on measles outbreak prediction. The model was retrained on the full 2017–2023 dataset to forecast district-level outbreak probabilities for 2024–2028, with 95% confidence intervals. Measles showed marked spatiotemporal heterogeneity shifting from northeastern districts during 2017–2018 toward the Dhaka periphery during 2021–2023. XGBoost achieved an AUC-ROC of 0.838 (accuracy 81.3%; F1 0.840; MCC 0.624), supporting risk-based district prioritisation rather than precise case-level prediction. Sociodemographic factors contributed most to the outbreak prediction(41.37%), followed by climatic(39.22%) and healthcare-related variables(19.41%). Vaccination coverage showed lower standalone importance, reflecting contextual risk modulation. Minimum temperature, household size, and poverty headcount were the dominant predictors of measles outbreak prediction. Sylhet, Kushtia, and Dhaka were most vulnerable districts of measles outbreak, while Khulna and Chittagong divisions maintained persistently elevated risk through 2028. Measles outbreak risk in Bangladesh is shaped by climatic and socioeconomic vulnerabilities alongside vaccination gaps. Explainable machine learning can support precision immunisation and targeted surveillance, while requiring cautious interpretation of forecast uncertainty.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles has re-emerged as a growing public health threat despite the availability of a safe and effective vaccine, and in Bangladesh, recurring outbreaks and widening immunization disparities underscore the urgent need for proactive district-level risk assessment.
Design and population
This study developed an explainable machine learning framework to identify drivers of and forecast district-level measles outbreak risk in Bangladesh using district-level measles surveillance data from 2017 to 2023.
Core methods
Eight machine learning classifiers were compared using temporal train/test splitting and nested cross-validation with F1-optimised decision thresholds, and SHapley Additive exPlanations quantified predictor contributions on measles outbreak prediction.
Main results
XGBoost achieved an AUC-ROC of 0.838 (accuracy 81.3%; F1 0.840; MCC 0.624), and sociodemographic factors contributed most to the outbreak prediction (41.37%), followed by climatic (39.22%) and healthcare-related variables (19.41%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating that measles outbreak risk in Bangladesh is shaped by climatic and socioeconomic vulnerabilities alongside vaccination gaps, and they highlight the novelty of using explainable machine learning to support precision immunisation and targeted surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted vaccination campaigns, precision immunisation strategies, and district-level risk prioritisation to mitigate measles outbreaks in Bangladesh.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges that the model supports risk-based district prioritisation rather than precise case-level prediction, and it requires cautious interpretation of forecast uncertainty, indicating moderate evidence strength.

使用基于试剂的建模方法比较评估 麻疹 免疫方案的有效性

Comparative evaluation of the effectiveness of measles immunization regimens using agent-based modeling methods
作者： A. A. Pozdnyakov, А. А. Pozdnyakov
摘要中文翻译实现和维持 麻疹 控制需要优化 麻疹 时间表,以考虑人口变化、免疫动态和感染 传播 模式。使用现代建模技术对不同免疫计划的长期有效性进行比较评估是一项相关的科学和实践任务。使用开发的基于主体的模拟模型对不同 麻疹 疫苗接种 时间表的长期流行病学影响进行比较评估。材料和方法。开发了基于空间隐式代理的随机模型,集成了人口学、迁移、母体和 疫苗 诱导的免疫以及特定年龄的空间接触的模块。在对 100,000 名药物进行的一系列 100 年模拟中,比较了三种方案:标准双剂量方案(SRN:12 个月和 6 年)、较早的首剂方案(SR9:9 个月和 6 年,严格的时效窗口)以及加强剂量方案(SR3:12 个月、6 和 18 年)。分析重点是长期平均 发病率、流行病间隔和病例年龄分布。与基线 SRN 场景相比,SR9 场景的长期平均 发病率 在统计上显着降低了 2.5 倍。SR9 与较长的流行间期间隔(SRN 为 6.12 年 vs. 5.75 年)、峰值 发病率 减少两倍以及更平滑的 暴发 动态相关。病例的年龄分布显示,与 SRN 和 SR3 相比,SR9 场景中一岁以下儿童和成人(18 岁以上)的 发病率 均显着减少。与标准方案相比,加强剂量 (SR3) 的引入并未导致长期平均 发病率 显着减少。与标准或延长加强计划相比,疫苗接种 日历的优化包括更早施用第一剂(9 个月)并严格控制 疫苗接种 的及时性,可以更显着和持续地减少 麻疹 发病率。获得的结果强调了细化 疫苗接种 起始年龄和调节 疫苗 给药时间作为长期流行病学控制的关键因素的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了优化 麻疹 疫苗接种 时间表的需要,以在人口变化、免疫动态和 传播 模式中维持控制,并确定了不同方案的长期有效性比较评估中的差距。
设计与对象
该研究使用基于空间隐式药物的随机模型来模拟 100,000 名药物人群中的 麻疹 传播 超过 100 年的时间,比较了三种 疫苗接种 方案:标准两剂 (SRN)、较早的首剂 (SR9) 和加强剂 (SR3)。
核心方法
基于代理的模型集成了人口统计、迁移、母亲和 疫苗 诱导的免疫力以及特定年龄的空间接触,以模拟 麻疹 传播 并评估 疫苗接种 场景的结果。
主要结果
与SRN相比,SR9时间表将长期平均发病率减少了2.5倍,将流行间期间隔从5.75年延长至6.12年,将峰值发病率减半,平滑了暴发动态,并降低了一岁以下儿童和18岁以上成人的发病率。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明较早的首剂给药和严格的及时性控制比标准或加强方案产生更好和更持久的 麻疹 控制,强调 麻疹 时机是长期流行病学影响的关键决定因素。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,首先细化 麻疹 的年龄并执行严格的及时性可以显着改善 麻疹 控制策略,为 疫苗接种 政策和未来的进度优化工作提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究对模拟人群以及关于接触模式和免疫动态的假设的依赖限制了其对现实世界环境的直接适用性,需要谨慎的解释和进一步的实证验证。

Comparative evaluation of the effectiveness of measles immunization regimens using agent-based modeling methods

Authors: A. A. Pozdnyakov, А. А. Pozdnyakov
Original abstract (English)
Achieving and sustaining measles control requires optimization of vaccination schedules that account for demographic changes, immunity dynamics, and infection transmission patterns. A comparative assessment of the long-term effectiveness of different immunization schedules using modern modeling techniques is a relevant scientific and practical task. To conduct a comparative assessment of the long-term epidemiological effect of different measles vaccination schedules using the developed agent-based simulation model. Materials and Methods . A spatially implicit agent-based stochastic model was developed, integrating modules for demography, migration, maternal and vaccine-induced immunity, and age-specific spatial contacts. In a series of 100-year simulations for a population of 100,000 agents, three scenarios were compared: a standard twodose schedule (SRN: 12 months and 6 years), a schedule with an earlier first dose (SR9: 9 months and 6 years, strict timeliness window), and a schedule with a booster dose (SR3: 12 months, 6 and 18 years). The analysis focused on the long-term average incidence, inter-epidemic intervals, and the age distribution of cases. The SR9 scenario demonstrated a statistically significant 2.5-fold reduction in the long-term average incidence compared to the baseline SRN scenario. SR9 was associated with longer inter-epidemic intervals (6.12 years vs. 5.75 years for SRN), a twofold reduction in peak incidence, and smoother outbreak dynamics. The age distribution of cases showed a significant reduction in incidence both among children under one year and among adults (18+) in the SR9 scenario compared to SRN and SR3. The introduction of a booster dose (SR3) did not lead to a significant reduction in long-term average incidence compared to the standard schedule. Optimization of the vaccination calendar involving an earlier administration of the first dose (9 months) combined with strict control over vaccination timeliness can provide a more significant and sustained reduction in measles incidence compared to standard or extended booster schedules. The obtained results highlight the importance of refining the age at vaccination initiation and regulating the timing of vaccine administration as key factors for long-term epidemiological control.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need to optimize measles vaccination schedules to sustain control amid demographic shifts, immunity dynamics, and transmission patterns, identifying a gap in comparative long-term effectiveness assessments of different regimens.
Design and population
The research used a spatially implicit agent-based stochastic model to simulate measles transmission over 100 years in a population of 100,000 agents, comparing three vaccination schedules: standard two-dose (SRN), earlier first dose (SR9), and booster-including (SR3).
Core methods
The agent-based model integrated demography, migration, maternal and vaccine-induced immunity, and age-specific spatial contacts to simulate measles transmission and evaluate outcomes across vaccination scenarios.
Main results
The SR9 schedule reduced long-term average incidence 2.5-fold compared to SRN, extended inter-epidemic intervals from 5.75 to 6.12 years, halved peak incidence, smoothed outbreak dynamics, and lowered incidence in children under one year and adults aged 18+.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence that earlier first-dose administration with strict timeliness control yields superior and more sustained measles control than standard or booster schedules, highlighting vaccination timing as a key determinant of long-term epidemiological impact.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that refining the age at first vaccination and enforcing strict timeliness could substantially improve measles control strategies, informing vaccination policy and future schedule optimization efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s reliance on a simulated population and assumptions about contact patterns and immunity dynamics limits direct applicability to real-world settings, warranting cautious interpretation and further empirical validation.

基于 CRISPR 的 麻疹病毒 双模式检测,适用于实验室和护理点应用

CRISPR-based dual-mode detection of measles virus for laboratory and point-of-care applications
作者： Wahedul Karim Ansari, Seung-Hyun Jung, Yeun-Jun Chung
摘要中文翻译摘要 快速、灵敏且可现场部署的诊断工具对于有效的 暴发 管理至关重要。为了满足这一需求,我们开发了一种一锅、无污染的检测方法,将逆转录环介导的等温扩增 (RT-LAMP) 与成簇规则间隔的短回文重复序列 (CRISPR-Cas12a) 相结合,用于快速、精确地检测 麻疹病毒 (MeV)。RT-LAMP 引物设计用于靶向 MeV 基因组 的保守区域。反应条件(包括温度、MgSO 4、脱氧核苷酸三磷酸 (dNTP) 和 Bst 聚合酶浓度)经过系统优化,以实现稳健的扩增。CRISPR RNA (crRNA) 旨在针对这些区域。CRISPR-Cas12a 试剂预装到管盖中。RT-LAMP 扩增后,将 CRISPR 试剂添加到试管中并通过手动摇动混合。CRISPR-Cas12a 表现出高度特异性的靶标识别和附带报告基因切割活性。评估了实验室环境中的荧光检测和护理点应用中的侧流读数。代表性灵敏度实验检测到每个反应的 MeV 模板低至 10 个拷贝,而 Probit 回归分析估计每个反应的 LoD 95 为 32.25 个拷贝,而单独 RT-LAMP 每个反应为 97.32 个拷贝。该检测显示出较高的分析特异性,未观察到与其他常见呼吸道病毒的交叉反应。MeV-CRISPR 测定在 45-50 分钟内完成。总体而言,该平台提供了快速、灵敏、特异的 MeV 检测,显示出 暴发 监测 现场应用的强大潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
有效的 麻疹病毒 (MeV) 暴发 管理需要快速、灵敏且可现场部署的诊断工具,但现有方法通常缺乏速度和便携性。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种将 RT-LAMP 与 CRISPR-Cas12a 结合的一锅法、无污染检测方法,用于快速、精确地检测 麻疹病毒。
核心方法
RT-LAMP 引物和 CRISPR RNA (crRNA) 旨在靶向 MeV 基因组 的保守区域,并优化反应条件以实现稳健扩增。将 CRISPR-Cas12a 试剂预装到管盖中,并在 RT-LAMP 扩增后手动混合。
主要结果
CRISPR-Cas12a 检测检测到每个反应低至 10 个拷贝的 MeV 模板,Probit 回归估计的 LoD95 为每个反应 32.25 个拷贝,优于单独的 RT-LAMP(每个反应 97.32 个拷贝)。该检测显示出很高的分析特异性,与其他常见呼吸道病毒没有交叉反应,并且在 45-50 分钟内完成。
解释与创新
作者将该平台解释为提供快速、灵敏和特异性的 MeV 检测,在 暴发 监测 中具有强大的现场应用潜力,强调了其作为集成 RT-LAMP 和 CRISPR-Cas12a 的双模式诊断的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
该平台可以通过在实验室和护理点环境中实现快速、灵敏和特异性的检测来增强 麻疹 暴发 监测,从而支持及时的公共卫生应对。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于分析性能,不包括临床验证或现场部署研究,需要对其现实世界的适用性进行谨慎解释。

CRISPR-based dual-mode detection of measles virus for laboratory and point-of-care applications

Authors: Wahedul Karim Ansari, Seung-Hyun Jung, Yeun-Jun Chung
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Rapid, sensitive, and field-deployable diagnostic tools are crucial for effective outbreak management. To address this need, we developed a one-pot, contamination-free assay integrating reverse transcription loop-mediated isothermal amplification (RT-LAMP) with clustered regularly interspaced short palindromic repeats (CRISPR-Cas12a) for the rapid and precise detection of the measles virus (MeV). RT-LAMP primers were designed to target conserved regions of the MeV genome. Reaction conditions—including temperature, MgSO 4, deoxynucleotide triphosphates (dNTPs), and Bst polymerase concentrations—were systematically optimized to achieve robust amplification. CRISPR RNA (crRNA) was designed to target these regions. CRISPR-Cas12a reagents were preloaded into the tube lid. Following RT-LAMP amplification, CRISPR reagents were added to the tubes and mixed by manual shaking. CRISPR-Cas12a demonstrated highly specific target recognition and collateral reporter cleavage activities. Fluorescence detection in laboratory settings and lateral-flow readouts in point-of-care applications were evaluated. Representative sensitivity experiments detected MeV templates down to 10 copies per reaction, while Probit regression analysis estimated LoD 95 of 32.25 copies per reaction, compared with 97.32 copies per reaction RT-LAMP alone. The assay demonstrated high analytical specificity, with no cross-reactivity observed against other common respiratory viruses. The MeV-CRISPR assay was completed within 45–50 min. Overall, this platform provided rapid, sensitive, and specific detection of MeV, showing strong potential for field applications in outbreak surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rapid, sensitive, and field-deployable diagnostic tools are needed for effective measles virus (MeV) outbreak management, yet existing methods often lack speed and portability.
Design and population
The study developed a one-pot, contamination-free assay integrating RT-LAMP with CRISPR-Cas12a for rapid and precise detection of the measles virus.
Core methods
RT-LAMP primers and CRISPR RNA (crRNA) were designed to target conserved regions of the MeV genome, with reaction conditions optimized for robust amplification. CRISPR-Cas12a reagents were preloaded into tube lids and mixed manually after RT-LAMP amplification.
Main results
The CRISPR-Cas12a assay detected MeV templates down to 10 copies per reaction, with a Probit regression-estimated LoD95 of 32.25 copies per reaction, outperforming RT-LAMP alone (97.32 copies per reaction). The assay showed high analytical specificity with no cross-reactivity against other common respiratory viruses and was completed within 45–50 minutes.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the platform as providing rapid, sensitive, and specific detection of MeV with strong potential for field applications in outbreak surveillance, highlighting its novelty as a dual-mode diagnostic integrating RT-LAMP and CRISPR-Cas12a.
Scientific and public-health significance
This platform could enhance measles outbreak surveillance by enabling rapid, sensitive, and specific detection in both laboratory and point-of-care settings, supporting timely public health responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to analytical performance and does not include clinical validation or field deployment studies, warranting cautious interpretation of its real-world applicability.

超融合 麻疹病毒 融合糖蛋白的融合后六螺旋束不稳定。

Hyperfusogenic Mutations Destabilize the Postfusion Six-Helix Bundle of the Measles Virus Fusion Glycoprotein.
作者： Nisansala Vithanage, Victor K Outlaw
摘要中文翻译I 类病毒融合蛋白的 宿主 膜和病毒包膜的融合是由融合后六螺旋束的组装驱动的,该六螺旋束是通过 N 端 (HR1) 和 C 端 (HR2) 七肽重复区域之间的反平行相互作用形成的。尽管已知 麻疹病毒 (MeV) 融合 (F) 糖蛋白这些区域中的 突变 会促进神经病理性和过度融合表型,但它们对融合后核心稳定性的影响尚未得到系统检查。在这里,我们结合肽生物物理学和 X 射线晶体学来探究 HR2 结构域内的 突变 如何影响融合后 6HB 组装,突变 存在于天然神经致病性 MeV 分离株(例如 L454W 和 N462K)和实验室产生的超融合变体(例如 L454M 和 T461A)中。圆二色性 (CD) 光谱表明,除了少数例外,这些 突变 会降低融合后核心稳定性,尽管它们与增强的融合活性有关。我们还报告了野生型 MeV 融合后核心的第一个晶体结构以及六种超融合变体的结构,从而能够对不稳定的分子基础进行高分辨率比较。结构分析表明,这些效应是由 HR2 内的空间堆积、氢键网络和螺旋稳定相互作用的局部扰动引起的,而整体 6HB 结构仍然保守。总之,这些结果表明,超融合性 突变 与融合后状态的稳定无关,而是与融合能量障碍减少而产生的超融合性模型一致,可能是通过对融合前稳定性或触发效率的影响。这些发现建立了控制卷曲螺旋组装的关键序列-结构-稳定性关系,并为基于 HR1 和 HR2 的融合抑制剂的设计提供了框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解释了为什么 麻疹病毒 (MeV) 融合糖蛋白七联体重复区域 (HR1/HR2) 中的某些 突变 会导致融合过度和神经病理性表型,尽管对融合后核心稳定性的影响尚不清楚。其目的是解决有关 突变 如何影响融合后六螺旋束 (6HB) 组装稳定性的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究结合肽生物物理学和 X 射线晶体学,检查 MeV 融合 (F) 糖蛋白 HR2 结构域中的 突变,包括来自天然神经致病分离株 (L454W、N462K) 和实验室产生的超融合变体 (L454M、T461A) 的 突变。
核心方法
使用圆二色性 (CD) 光谱来评估融合后核心稳定性,并确定野生型 MeV 融合后核心和六种超融合变体的高分辨率晶体结构,以比较 6HB 内的分子相互作用和空间堆积。
主要结果
CD 光谱表明,除了少数例外,突变 降低了融合后核心稳定性,尽管它们与增强的融合活性有关。结构分析揭示了 HR2 内的空间堆积、氢键网络和螺旋稳定相互作用的局部扰动,而整体 6HB 结构保持保守。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为超融合性 突变 破坏融合后状态稳定的证据,这与超融合性是由于融合能量障碍减少而产生的模型一致,可能是通过对融合前稳定性或触发效率的影响。该研究提供了野生型 MeV 融合后核心的第一个晶体结构和六种超融合变体的结构,建立了关键的序列-结构-稳定性关系。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为针对 麻疹病毒 进入的基于 HR1 和 HR2 的融合抑制剂的设计提供了信息,并可能指导减轻与特定 麻疹病毒 相关的过度融合或神经病理表型的策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的优势在于其生物物理和结构方法的整合,但它并没有直接评估预融合稳定性或触发效率,从而部分推断了与超融合性的机制联系。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于体外模型和结构比较,而没有功能融合测定或体内验证。

Hyperfusogenic Mutations Destabilize the Postfusion Six-Helix Bundle of the Measles Virus Fusion Glycoprotein.

Authors: Nisansala Vithanage, Victor K Outlaw
Original abstract (English)
Fusion of the host membrane and viral envelope by class I viral fusion proteins is driven by the assembly of a postfusion six-helix bundle formed through antiparallel interactions between N-terminal (HR1) and C-terminal (HR2) heptad-repeat regions. Although mutations in these regions of the measles virus (MeV) fusion (F) glycoprotein are known to promote neuropathogenic and hyperfusogenic phenotypes, their effects on postfusion core stability have not been systematically examined. Here, we combine peptide biophysics and X-ray crystallography to interrogate how mutations within the HR2 domain, present in native neuropathogenic MeV isolates (e.g., L454W and N462K) and laboratory-generated hyperfusogenic variants (e.g., L454M and T461A), influence postfusion 6HB assembly. Circular dichroism (CD) spectroscopy reveals that, with few exceptions, these mutations decrease postfusion core stability, despite their association with enhanced fusion activity. We also report the first crystal structure of the wild-type MeV postfusion core as well as structures of six hyperfusogenic variants, enabling high-resolution comparison of the molecular basis of destabilization. Structural analysis shows that these effects arise from localized perturbations to steric packing, hydrogen bonding networks, and helix-stabilizing interactions within HR2, while the overall 6HB architecture remains conserved. Together, these results indicate that hyperfusogenic mutations are not associated with stabilization of the postfusion state and are instead consistent with models in which hyperfusogenicity arises from a reduction in the energetic barrier to fusion, potentially through effects on prefusion stability or triggering efficiency. These findings establish key sequence-structure-stability relationships governing coiled-coil assembly and provide a framework for the design of HR1- and HR2-based fusion inhibitors.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed why certain mutations in the measles virus (MeV) fusion glycoprotein’s heptad-repeat regions (HR1/HR2) cause hyperfusogenic and neuropathogenic phenotypes despite unclear effects on postfusion core stability. It aimed to resolve a knowledge gap about how these mutations influence the stability of the postfusion six-helix bundle (6HB) assembly.
Design and population
The research combined peptide biophysics and X-ray crystallography to examine mutations in the HR2 domain of the MeV fusion (F) glycoprotein, including those from native neuropathogenic isolates (L454W, N462K) and laboratory-generated hyperfusogenic variants (L454M, T461A).
Core methods
Circular dichroism (CD) spectroscopy was used to assess postfusion core stability, and high-resolution crystal structures of the wild-type MeV postfusion core and six hyperfusogenic variants were determined to compare molecular interactions and steric packing within the 6HB.
Main results
CD spectroscopy showed that, with few exceptions, the mutations decreased postfusion core stability despite their association with enhanced fusion activity. Structural analysis revealed localized perturbations to steric packing, hydrogen bonding networks, and helix-stabilizing interactions within HR2, while the overall 6HB architecture remained conserved.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that hyperfusogenic mutations destabilize the postfusion state, consistent with models where hyperfusogenicity arises from reduced energetic barriers to fusion, potentially through effects on prefusion stability or triggering efficiency. The study provides the first crystal structure of the wild-type MeV postfusion core and structures of six hyperfusogenic variants, establishing key sequence-structure-stability relationships.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results inform the design of HR1- and HR2-based fusion inhibitors targeting measles virus entry and may guide strategies to mitigate hyperfusogenic or neuropathogenic phenotypes associated with specific mutations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s strength lies in its integration of biophysical and structural methods, but it does not directly assess prefusion stability or triggering efficiency, leaving mechanistic links to hyperfusogenicity partially inferred. The evidence is moderate, as it relies on in vitro models and structural comparisons without functional fusion assays or in vivo validation.

2018-2024 年武装冲突对埃塞俄比亚提格雷 麻疹 监测 和零剂量 疫苗接种 状态的影响

Effect of armed conflict on measles surveillance and zero-dose vaccination status in Tigray, Ethiopia, 2018–2024
作者： Habtamu Milkias Wolde, Ali Raza, Youngoh Bae, Seung Won Lee
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 武装冲突扰乱卫生系统,破坏常规免疫和疾病 监测。这些干扰可能会延迟 暴发 检测,并使人群易感性在不被注意的情况下累积。本研究评估了 2020-2022 年埃塞俄比亚提格雷冲突对 麻疹 流行病学 的影响,重点关注 麻疹 报告、病例年龄分布和 疫苗接种 状态。方法 我们对埃塞俄比亚(2018-2024 年;n = 69,866)基于全国病例的 麻疹 监测 数据进行了回顾性纵向分析。研究了冲突前(2018-2019)、冲突高峰(2020-2022)和冲突后恢复(2023-2024)期间麻疹、年龄分布和零剂量疫苗接种状态的变化。双向方差分析检查了各阶段感染年龄的区域差异。使用冲突前时期作为参考并调整年龄和性别,多变量逻辑回归估计了未接种疫苗(零剂量)的确诊 麻疹 病例的调整优势比 (aOR)。监测 质量是使用人口统计数据完整性进行评估的。结果 在冲突高峰期间,提格雷报告的 麻疹 病例数下降至全国报告病例数的 0.01%,与 麻疹 几乎全部崩溃的情况一致。敌对行动结束后,儿科出现了明显的转变,提格雷的感染中位年龄从冲突期间的 24.0 岁下降到冲突后的 5.0 岁 (p < 0.0001),这种模式在其他地区没有观察到。与冲突前基线相比,冲突高峰期间确诊的 麻疹 病例零剂量的几率明显较高(aOR:71.43;95% CI:14.1-1000.0),并且在冲突后恢复期间保持较高水平(aOR:2.49;95% CI:1.17-5.52)。在冲突高峰期间,提格雷 50% 的确诊病例缺乏按性别分列的数据。结论 这种冲突与 麻疹 麻疹 几乎完全崩溃、报告病例年龄分布的显着变化以及零剂量 麻疹 病例比例增加有关。这些发现凸显了在冲突后恢复期间需要有弹性的 监测 系统和有针对性的追赶 疫苗接种。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
武装冲突扰乱卫生系统并破坏常规免疫和疾病 监测,可能会延迟 暴发 检测并导致易感性在不被注意的情况下累积。本研究评估了 2020-2022 年埃塞俄比亚提格雷冲突对 麻疹 流行病学 的影响,重点关注 麻疹 报告、病例年龄分布和 疫苗接种 状态。
设计与对象
对埃塞俄比亚(2018-2024 年;n = 69,866)的国家基于案例的 麻疹 监测 数据进行了回顾性纵向分析。对冲突前(2018-2019)、冲突高峰(2020-2022)和冲突后恢复(2023-2024)期间麻疹、年龄分布和零剂量疫苗接种状态的变化进行了检查。
核心方法
双向方差分析检查了各阶段感染年龄的区域差异。使用冲突前时期作为参考并调整年龄和性别,多变量逻辑回归估计了未接种疫苗(零剂量)的确诊 麻疹 病例的调整优势比 (aOR)。监测 质量是使用人口统计数据完整性进行评估的。
主要结果
在冲突高峰期间,提格雷报告的 麻疹 病例下降至全国报告病例的 0.01%,与 麻疹 几乎全部崩溃的情况一致。敌对行动结束后,儿科出现了明显的转变,提格雷感染的中位年龄从冲突期间的 24.0 岁下降到冲突后时期的 5.0 岁(p < 0.0001)。与冲突前基线相比,冲突高峰期间确诊的 麻疹 病例零剂量的几率明显较高(aOR:71.43;95% CI:14.1-1000.0),并且在冲突后恢复期间保持较高水平(aOR:2.49;95% CI:1.17-5.52)。
解释与创新
此次冲突与 麻疹 麻疹 几乎完全崩溃、报告病例年龄分布的显着变化以及零剂量 麻疹 病例比例增加有关。这些发现凸显了在冲突后恢复期间需要有弹性的 监测 系统和有针对性的追赶 疫苗接种。
科研与公卫意义
研究表明,武装冲突会严重扰乱 麻疹 监测 和 疫苗接种 计划,导致未被发现的 暴发 和易受影响人群的积累。它强调了在冲突后环境中加强 监测 和实施追赶 疫苗接种 战略的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于 监测 数据,这些数据在冲突期间可能不完整或有偏见,特别是在冲突高峰期间,50% 的确诊病例缺乏按性别分列的数据。尽管存在这些限制,但由于冲突阶段与观察到的 监测 质量、年龄分布和 疫苗接种 状态变化之间存在明显的时间关联,证据仍然很充分。

Effect of armed conflict on measles surveillance and zero-dose vaccination status in Tigray, Ethiopia, 2018–2024

Authors: Habtamu Milkias Wolde, Ali Raza, Youngoh Bae, Seung Won Lee
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Armed conflict disrupts health systems, undermining routine immunization and disease surveillance. These disruptions can delay outbreak detection and allow population susceptibility to accumulate unnoticed. This study assessed the impact of the 2020–2022 conflict in Tigray, Ethiopia, on measles epidemiology, focusing on surveillance reporting, age distribution of cases, and vaccination status. Methods We conducted a retrospective longitudinal analysis of national case-based measles surveillance data from Ethiopia (2018–2024; n = 69,866). Changes in measles surveillance, age distribution, and zero-dose vaccination status across the pre-conflict (2018–2019), conflict peak (2020–2022), and post-conflict recovery (2023–2024) periods were examined. Two-way analysis of variance examined regional differences in age at infection across phases. Multivariable logistic regression estimated adjusted odds ratios (aORs) for confirmed measles cases being unvaccinated (zero-dose), using the pre-conflict period as the reference and adjusting for age and sex. Surveillance quality was assessed using demographic data completeness. Results During the conflict peak, reported measles cases from Tigray declined to 0.01% of nationally reported cases, consistent with near-total surveillance collapse. After hostilities ended, a marked pediatric shift emerged, with the median age of infection in Tigray declining from 24.0 years during the conflict to 5.0 years in the post-conflict period ( p < 0.0001), a pattern not observed in other regions. Compared with the pre-conflict baseline, the odds that a confirmed measles case was zero-dose were substantially higher during the conflict peak (aOR: 71.43; 95% CI: 14.1–1000.0) and remained elevated during post-conflict recovery (aOR: 2.49; 95% CI: 1.17–5.52). During the conflict peak, 50% of confirmed cases in Tigray lacked sex-disaggregated data. Conclusion The conflict was associated with a near-complete collapse of measles surveillance, marked changes in the age distribution of reported cases, and an increased proportion of zero-dose measles cases. These findings highlight the need for resilient surveillance systems and targeted catch-up vaccination during post-conflict recovery.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Armed conflict disrupts health systems and undermines routine immunization and disease surveillance, potentially delaying outbreak detection and allowing susceptibility to accumulate unnoticed. This study assessed the impact of the 2020–2022 conflict in Tigray, Ethiopia, on measles epidemiology, focusing on surveillance reporting, age distribution of cases, and vaccination status.
Design and population
A retrospective longitudinal analysis of national case-based measles surveillance data from Ethiopia (2018–2024; n = 69,866) was conducted. Changes in measles surveillance, age distribution, and zero-dose vaccination status were examined across the pre-conflict (2018–2019), conflict peak (2020–2022), and post-conflict recovery (2023–2024) periods.
Core methods
Two-way analysis of variance examined regional differences in age at infection across phases. Multivariable logistic regression estimated adjusted odds ratios (aORs) for confirmed measles cases being unvaccinated (zero-dose), using the pre-conflict period as the reference and adjusting for age and sex. Surveillance quality was assessed using demographic data completeness.
Main results
During the conflict peak, reported measles cases from Tigray declined to 0.01% of nationally reported cases, consistent with near-total surveillance collapse. After hostilities ended, a marked pediatric shift emerged, with the median age of infection in Tigray declining from 24.0 years during the conflict to 5.0 years in the post-conflict period (p < 0.0001). Compared with the pre-conflict baseline, the odds that a confirmed measles case was zero-dose were substantially higher during the conflict peak (aOR: 71.43; 95% CI: 14.1–1000.0) and remained elevated during post-conflict recovery (aOR: 2.49; 95% CI: 1.17–5.52).
Interpretation and novelty
The conflict was associated with a near-complete collapse of measles surveillance, marked changes in the age distribution of reported cases, and an increased proportion of zero-dose measles cases. These findings highlight the need for resilient surveillance systems and targeted catch-up vaccination during post-conflict recovery.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study demonstrates that armed conflict can severely disrupt measles surveillance and vaccination programs, leading to undetected outbreaks and accumulation of susceptible populations. It underscores the importance of strengthening surveillance and implementing catch-up vaccination strategies in post-conflict settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on surveillance data, which may be incomplete or biased during conflict, particularly with 50% of confirmed cases lacking sex-disaggregated data during the conflict peak. Despite these limitations, the evidence is strong due to the clear temporal association between conflict phases and observed changes in surveillance quality, age distribution, and vaccination status.

全国 18 岁以下儿童和青少年 麻疹 疫苗接种、验证、追赶和应对运动

National campaign for measles vaccination, verification, catch-up, and response among children and adolescents under 18 years
作者： Sana El Fadeli, El Faiza Abouraicha, Hicham Mejdouli, Morad Kaddouri, Mohammed Al-Zharani, Fahd A. Nasr, Ashraf Ahmed Qurtam, Abdelkabir Chamhari, Abdelaziz Ait Melloul, Zakaria Ait Lahcen
摘要中文翻译2023年,摩洛哥经历了全国性的麻疹 暴发。2023年10月至2025年3月,在全国范围内开展了针对18岁以下儿童青少年的“麻疹 疫苗接种、核查、追赶、响应”活动。本研究旨在评估并全面描述该活动对 麻疹 疫苗接种 覆盖范围的影响,强调需要解决 COVID-19 后免疫接种中断问题。使用国家活动数据估计验证率、不完整性水平和追赶覆盖率,并使用描述性统计、箱线图和主成分分析(PCA)评估区域差异。将结果与有关控制 麻疹 的国际文献进行了比较,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC)。整体活动覆盖率较高,平均验证率为96.64%,尤其是东方地区,验证率超过100%,可能反映了分母低估和/或跨境疫苗接种流动。然而,地区间追赶率存在显着差异,从 Eddakhla-Oued Eddahab 的 79.05% 到 Guelmim-Oued Noun 的低至 16.12%。这些差异可能反映了各地区地理可达性、卫生系统能力和社会人口因素的差异。全国不完整率为15.69%,改善空间很大。COVID-19 大流行造成的服务中断导致活动前免疫接种率下降。虽然该活动与改进验证和加强 麻疹 疫苗接种 覆盖范围有关,但区域差异和不完整的覆盖范围仍然存在。为了实现可持续的 麻疹 控制,摩洛哥需要借鉴世界其他地区的最佳实践,采取有针对性的干预措施,包括基于学校的 麻疹、数字提醒系统和加强社区参与,以实现消除目标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2023 年,摩洛哥经历了全国范围内的 麻疹 暴发,从而产生了在 COVID-19 相关中断后恢复 疫苗接种 覆盖范围的紧迫公共卫生需求。这项研究的动机是运动前覆盖率与消除目标之间的差距,旨在评估全国运动对 麻疹 疫苗接种 覆盖率的影响。
设计与对象
2023年10月至2025年3月,摩洛哥针对18岁以下儿童青少年开展了全国“麻疹、疫苗接种、核查、追赶和应对”活动。使用国家活动数据和区域分析来评估该活动的覆盖范围和差异。
核心方法
使用国家活动数据估计验证率、不完整性水平和追赶覆盖率,并使用描述性统计、箱线图和主成分分析(PCA)评估区域差异。结果与低收入和中等收入国家 麻疹 控制的国际文献进行了比较。
主要结果
活动整体覆盖率较高,平均验证率高达96.64%,尤其是东方地区,验证率超过100%。地区间追赶率存在显着差异,Eddakhla-Oued Eddahab 为 79.05%,Guelmim-Oued Noun 为 16.12%,全国不完整率为 15.69%。
解释与创新
该活动与改进验证和加强 麻疹 疫苗接种 覆盖范围相关,但地区差异和覆盖不完整的情况仍然存在。作者强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施,例如基于学校的 疫苗接种 和数字提醒系统,以实现消除目标。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果通过确定优先区域并建议量身定制的干预措施来解决 麻疹 的地理、卫生系统和社会人口障碍,为摩洛哥的 麻疹 消除战略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究对竞选数据和描述性分析的依赖限制了因果推断和普遍性,并且使用主成分分析来解决区域差异引入了方法论假设。由于这些限制以及缺乏对照组或长期随访,证据是中等的。

National campaign for measles vaccination, verification, catch-up, and response among children and adolescents under 18 years

Authors: Sana El Fadeli, El Faiza Abouraicha, Hicham Mejdouli, Morad Kaddouri, Mohammed Al-Zharani, Fahd A. Nasr, Ashraf Ahmed Qurtam, Abdelkabir Chamhari, Abdelaziz Ait Melloul, Zakaria Ait Lahcen
Original abstract (English)
In 2023, Morocco experienced a nationwide measles outbreak. From October 2023 to March 2025, a national campaign of measles vaccination, verification, catch-up, and response was launched, targeting children and adolescents under 18 years of age. This study aims to assess and comprehensively describe the campaign’s impact on measles vaccination coverage, highlighting the need to address post-COVID-19 immunization disruptions. Verification rates, incompleteness levels, and catch-up coverage were estimated using national campaign data, and regional disparities were assessed using descriptive statistics, box plots, and principal component analysis (PCA). Results were compared with the international literature on efforts to control measles, particularly in low-and middle-income countries (LMICs). The overall campaign coverage was high, with an average verification rate of 96.64%, particularly strong in the Oriental region, where the rate exceeded 100%, likely reflecting denominator underestimation and/or cross-boundary vaccination flows. However, substantial inter-regional disparities were observed in catch-up rates, ranging from 79.05% in Eddakhla-Oued Eddahab to as low as 16.12% in Guelmim-Oued Noun. These disparities may reflect differences in geographic accessibility, health system capacity, and sociodemographic factors across regions. The national incompleteness rate stood at 15.69%, indicating significant room for improvement. Service disruptions resulting from the COVID-19 pandemic contributed to the decline in immunization rates before the campaign. While the campaign was associated with improved verification and strengthened measles vaccination coverage, regional disparities and incomplete coverage persisted. In the quest for sustainable measles control, Morocco will require targeted interventions, including school-based vaccination, digital reminder systems, and enhanced community engagement, to achieve its elimination goals, informed by best practices from other parts of the world.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
In 2023, Morocco experienced a nationwide measles outbreak, creating a pressing public-health need to restore vaccination coverage after COVID-19–related disruptions. The study was motivated by the gap between pre-campaign coverage and elimination targets and aimed to evaluate the national campaign’s impact on measles vaccination coverage.
Design and population
From October 2023 to March 2025, Morocco conducted a national campaign of measles vaccination, verification, catch-up, and response targeting children and adolescents under 18 years of age. The campaign’s coverage and disparities were assessed using national campaign data and regional analyses.
Core methods
Verification rates, incompleteness levels, and catch-up coverage were estimated using national campaign data, and regional disparities were assessed with descriptive statistics, box plots, and principal component analysis (PCA). Results were compared with international literature on measles control in low- and middle-income countries.
Main results
The overall campaign coverage was high, with an average verification rate of 96.64%, particularly strong in the Oriental region where the rate exceeded 100%. Substantial inter-regional disparities were observed in catch-up rates, ranging from 79.05% in Eddakhla-Oued Eddahab to 16.12% in Guelmim-Oued Noun, and the national incompleteness rate stood at 15.69%.
Interpretation and novelty
The campaign was associated with improved verification and strengthened measles vaccination coverage, but regional disparities and incomplete coverage persisted. The authors highlight the need for targeted interventions such as school-based vaccination and digital reminder systems to achieve elimination goals.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform Morocco’s measles elimination strategy by identifying priority regions and recommending tailored interventions to address geographic, health-system, and sociodemographic barriers to vaccination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s reliance on campaign data and descriptive analyses limits causal inference and generalisability, and the use of PCA for regional disparities introduces methodological assumptions. The evidence is moderate due to these constraints and the lack of control groups or long-term follow-up.

正交转录系统能够高效拯救基于 麻疹病毒 的 疫苗 媒介 针对多种病原体

An orthogonal transcription system enables high-efficiency rescue of measles virus-based vaccine vectors against multiple pathogens
作者： Zihan Ma, Weijun Wang, Qiuli Lou, Hai Jiang, Yanhui Xiang, Wenhui Zhang, Xue Tian, Yu Fan, Wen Yin, Chunbo Lou
摘要中文翻译重组 麻疹病毒 (rMeV) 媒介 由于其安全性、稳定性和强大的免疫原性,是针对新出现的传染病的 疫苗 开发的有前途的平台。然而,传统的 rMeV 救援系统经常表现出低效率,从而限制了其可扩展性和吞吐量。在这项研究中,我们开发了一个模块化、无辅助病毒且高效的救援平台,该平台基于正交转录系统,利用正交启动子和与 mRNA 加帽酶融合的工程化 RNA 聚合酶。这种创新系统促进了基因组和辅助成分的稳健细胞质制造,消除了辅助病毒共感染(例如改良牛痘病毒)的需要,并且相对于传统的救援方法将救援效率提高了 50 倍以上。利用这项技术,我们成功地从流感病毒、铜绿假单胞菌和布鲁氏菌属中生成了六种基于 rMeV 的 疫苗 候选抗原,证明了该平台的多功能性。所有获救的 疫苗 候选者均表现出稳定的转基因表达、持续的复制和强大的抗原产生。对金色叙利亚仓鼠的免疫研究证实,疫苗 候选药物可引发高滴度的中和抗体和抗原特异性抗体,且没有任何可观察到的副作用。这些结果表明,我们的基于正交转录的平台促进了 rMeV 媒介 的高效、安全生产,并为 疫苗 候选物的快速开发提供了概念验证方法框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
重组 麻疹病毒 (rMeV) 媒介 是有前景的 疫苗 平台,但传统救援系统效率低下,限制了可扩展性。这项研究解决了对高效救援平台的需求,以实现快速开发基于 rMeV 的 疫苗 候选药物来对抗新出现的病原体。
设计与对象
该研究开发并验证了一个模块化、无辅助病毒的救援平台,该平台使用具有正交启动子的正交转录系统以及与 mRNA 加帽酶融合的工程化 RNA 聚合酶。该平台用于生成六种基于 rMeV 的 疫苗 候选抗原,针对流感病毒、铜绿假单胞菌和布鲁氏菌属。
核心方法
正交转录系统能够在细胞质中制造基因组和辅助成分,而无需辅助病毒共感染,与传统方法相比,救援效率提高了 50 倍以上。对获救的 疫苗 候选者进行转基因表达稳定性、复制能力和抗原产生的评估。
主要结果
所有六种基于 rMeV 的 疫苗 候选物均表现出稳定的转基因表达、持续的复制和强大的抗原产生。金色叙利亚仓鼠的免疫引发了高滴度的中和抗体和抗原特异性抗体,且没有观察到不良反应。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明基于正交转录的平台能够高效、安全地生产 rMeV 媒介,为快速 疫苗 候选药物开发提供概念验证方法框架。新颖之处在于无辅助病毒的高效救援系统,消除了对改良痘苗病毒合并感染的依赖。
科研与公卫意义
该平台可以加速针对多种病原体的基于 rMeV 的 疫苗 的开发,支持大流行的防范并减少对传统救援方法的依赖。它还可以提高 疫苗 的制造可扩展性和应对新出现的传染病威胁的吞吐量。
局限与证据强度
该研究在体外和小动物模型中进行了概念验证,这可能无法完全预测人类的安全性或有效性。虽然救援效率显着提高,但该平台对其他病原体的通用性和长期稳定性需要进一步验证。由于动物试验范围有限且缺乏人体数据,证据强度中等。

An orthogonal transcription system enables high-efficiency rescue of measles virus-based vaccine vectors against multiple pathogens

Authors: Zihan Ma, Weijun Wang, Qiuli Lou, Hai Jiang, Yanhui Xiang, Wenhui Zhang, Xue Tian, Yu Fan, Wen Yin, Chunbo Lou
Original abstract (English)
Recombinant measles virus (rMeV) vectors are promising platforms for vaccine development against emerging infectious diseases due to their safety, stability, and potent immunogenicity. However, conventional rMeV rescue systems frequently exhibit low efficiency, thereby constraining their scalability and throughput. In this study, we developed a modular, helper-virus-free and high-efficiency rescue platform based on an orthogonal transcription system utilizing orthogonal promoters and engineered RNA polymerases fused to an mRNA capping enzyme. This innovative system facilitated robust cytoplasmic manufacture of both genomic and auxiliary components, eliminating the need for helper virus co-infection (such as modified vaccinia virus) and enhancing rescue efficiency by more than 50-fold relative to traditional rescue approaches. Utilizing this technology, we demonstrated the versatility of the platform by successfully generating six rMeV-based vaccine antigen candidates from influenza virus, Pseudomonas aeruginosa , and Brucella spp. All rescued vaccine candidates exhibited stable transgene expression, sustained replication, and strong antigen production. Immunization studies in golden Syrian hamsters verified that the vaccine candidates elicited high titers of neutralizing and antigen-specific antibodies without any observable adverse effects. These results demonstrate that our orthogonal transcription-based platform facilitates the efficient and safe production of rMeV vectors and provides a proof-of-concept methodological framework for the rapid development of vaccine candidates.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Recombinant measles virus (rMeV) vectors are promising vaccine platforms but conventional rescue systems suffer from low efficiency, limiting scalability. This study addressed the need for a high-efficiency rescue platform to enable rapid development of rMeV-based vaccine candidates against emerging pathogens.
Design and population
The study developed and validated a modular, helper-virus-free rescue platform using an orthogonal transcription system with orthogonal promoters and engineered RNA polymerases fused to an mRNA capping enzyme. The platform was applied to generate six rMeV-based vaccine antigen candidates targeting influenza virus, Pseudomonas aeruginosa, and Brucella spp.
Core methods
The orthogonal transcription system enabled cytoplasmic manufacture of genomic and auxiliary components without helper virus co-infection, enhancing rescue efficiency by more than 50-fold compared to traditional approaches. Rescued vaccine candidates were assessed for transgene expression stability, replication capacity, and antigen production.
Main results
All six rMeV-based vaccine candidates demonstrated stable transgene expression, sustained replication, and strong antigen production. Immunization in golden Syrian hamsters elicited high titers of neutralizing and antigen-specific antibodies without observable adverse effects.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating that the orthogonal transcription-based platform enables efficient and safe production of rMeV vectors, providing a proof-of-concept methodological framework for rapid vaccine candidate development. The novelty lies in the helper-virus-free, high-efficiency rescue system that eliminates reliance on modified vaccinia virus co-infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
This platform could accelerate the development of rMeV-based vaccines against multiple pathogens, supporting pandemic preparedness and reducing reliance on traditional rescue methods. It may also improve vaccine manufacturing scalability and throughput for emerging infectious disease threats.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study demonstrates proof-of-concept in vitro and in a small animal model, which may not fully predict human safety or efficacy. While rescue efficiency was markedly improved, the platform's generalizability across additional pathogens and long-term stability require further validation. The evidence strength is moderate due to the limited scope of animal testing and absence of human data.

2026 年孟加拉国 麻疹 暴发:对免疫差距和护理缺陷的批评

The 2026 Measles Outbreak in Bangladesh: A Critique of Immunity Gaps and Care Deficiencies
作者： Syed Abu Hasanth
摘要中文翻译孟加拉国在 5 月中旬达到顶峰的严重 麻疹 麻疹 中进入 2026 年。本文分析了公共卫生部门对 暴发 的反应,并评估了政府对危机的管理。卫生专业人员和 麻疹 专家将 麻疹 归因于常规免疫积累的差距、公共卫生基础设施薄弱、儿童营养状况不佳、维生素 A 普遍缺乏以及孕产妇在怀孕和哺乳期间营养不良。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了孟加拉国为何在 2026 年初经历了严重的 麻疹 暴发 以及造成其严重程度的因素。它审查公共卫生应对措施并评估政府对危机的管理。
设计与对象
文章分析了孟加拉国2026年麻疹 暴发,重点关注公共卫生应对和危机管理。它批评政府对 暴发 的处理方式及其根本原因。
核心方法
该分析综合了专家的归因,以确定常规免疫接种的差距、公共卫生基础设施的薄弱、儿童营养状况不佳、维生素 A 普遍缺乏以及孕产妇在怀孕和母乳喂养期间营养不良等因素。
主要结果
麻疹 于 2026 年 5 月中旬达到顶峰,卫生专业人员和 麻疹 专家将其严重性归因于累积的免疫缺口和全身护理缺陷。
解释与创新
作者将 暴发 解释为卫生系统和营养方面长期存在的结构性弱点的结果,强调需要持续改善免疫覆盖率和公共卫生基础设施。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了解决常规免疫差距、加强公共卫生基础设施以及改善孕产妇和儿童营养以预防未来 麻疹 暴发 的重要性。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于专家归因和描述性分析,没有原始数据收集或定量评估,降低了因果推论的强度。

The 2026 Measles Outbreak in Bangladesh: A Critique of Immunity Gaps and Care Deficiencies

Authors: Syed Abu Hasanth
Original abstract (English)
Bangladesh entered 2026 amid a severe measles outbreak that peaked in mid-May. This article analyses the public health response to the outbreak and evaluates the government's management of the crisis. Health professionals and measles experts have attributed the outbreak to accumulated gaps in routine immunisation, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses why Bangladesh experienced a severe measles outbreak in early 2026 and what factors contributed to its magnitude. It examines the public health response and evaluates the government's management of the crisis.
Design and population
The article analyses the 2026 measles outbreak in Bangladesh, focusing on the public health response and crisis management. It critiques the government's handling of the outbreak and its underlying causes.
Core methods
The analysis synthesizes expert attributions to identify gaps in routine immunization, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding as contributing factors.
Main results
The outbreak peaked in mid-May 2026, with health professionals and measles experts attributing its severity to accumulated immunity gaps and systemic care deficiencies.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the outbreak as a consequence of long-standing structural weaknesses in health systems and nutrition, highlighting the need for sustained improvements in immunization coverage and public health infrastructure.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of addressing routine immunization gaps, strengthening public health infrastructure, and improving maternal and child nutrition to prevent future measles outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to expert attributions and descriptive analysis without primary data collection or quantitative assessment, reducing the strength of causal inferences.

麻疹 血清学 计划:提高服务提供商对 Rubeola 血清学 筛查合规性的质量改进干预措施

The Measles Serology Initiative: A Quality Improvement Intervention to Increase Provider Compliance with Rubeola Serology Screening
作者： Erika S Martinez
摘要中文翻译目的 该质量改进 (QI) 项目的目的是使提供者在门诊妇女健康诊所产前检查期间对风疹 IgG 血清学 筛查的依从性从 63.3% 的基线依从率提高 10%。背景 风疹仍然是一种高度传染性的病毒感染,对未免疫的孕妇及其新生儿构成健康风险。随着风疹病例的增加以及确定怀孕期间免疫状态的重要性,一致的产前筛查实践至关重要。基线数据表明,对 Rubeola IgG 筛查建议的遵守情况不一致,强调需要通过工作流程干预来提高提供者的合规性。方法论 以爱荷华模型和 Donabedian 框架为指导的前后 QI 设计评估了包括提供者教育、可视化工作流程提示以及审核和反馈在内的干预措施。干预前数据包括 2025 年 10 月至 2026 年 1 月的 2,568 条图表审查。干预后数据包括 2026 年 1 月至 4 月的 2,173 条图表审查。通过比较干预前后的依从率来分析数据。项目限制包括由于对 Epic 报告的访问有限而手动提取图表。结果 提供者对风疹 IgG 筛查的依从性从干预前的 63.3% 增加到干预后的 75.1%,增加了 11.8%。启示 提供者教育、视觉提示以及审核和反馈的实施表明,基于工作流程的干预措施可以提高对基于证据的 血清学 筛查实践的遵守率。这些策略识别非免疫个体,在激增期间减少社区 传播,保护弱势群体,并避免设施 传播 的运营成本。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决产前检查期间对风疹 IgG 血清学 筛查依从性不一致的问题,这对未免疫的怀孕患者及其新生儿构成健康风险。
设计与对象
采用前后质量改进(QI)设计来评估门诊妇女健康诊所的干预措施。干预前数据包括2025年10月至2026年1月的2,568次图表审查,而干预后数据包括2026年1月至2026年4月的2,173次图表审查。
核心方法
该研究采用了提供者教育、可视化工作流程提示以及审核和反馈作为爱荷华模型和多纳贝迪安框架指导下的干预措施。通过比较干预前和干预后的依从率来分析数据。
主要结果
提供者对风疹 IgG 筛查的依从性从干预前的 63.3% 增加到干预后的 75.1%,增加了 11.8%。
解释与创新
基于工作流程的干预措施的实施证明了一种有效的方法,可以提高对鲁贝拉循证 血清学 筛查实践的依从性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,有针对性的工作流程干预可以加强风疹的产前筛查,从而降低非免疫妊娠患者及其新生儿的 传播 风险。
局限与证据强度
由于 Epic 报告的访问受到限制,该研究受到手动图表提取的限制,这可能会引入潜在的偏差。证据强度中等。

The Measles Serology Initiative: A Quality Improvement Intervention to Increase Provider Compliance with Rubeola Serology Screening

Authors: Erika S Martinez
Original abstract (English)
Purpose The purpose of this quality improvement (QI) project was to increase provider adherence to Rubeola IgG serology screening during prenatal visits at an outpatient women’s health clinic by 10% from a baseline adherence rate of 63.3%. Background Rubeola remains a highly contagious viral infection that poses health risks for nonimmune pregnant patients and their newborns. With increasing Rubeola cases and the importance of identifying immunity status during pregnancy, consistent prenatal screening practices are essential. Baseline data demonstrated inconsistent adherence to Rubeola IgG screening recommendations, highlighting the need for workflow interventions to improve provider compliance. Methodology A pre-post QI design guided by the Iowa Model and Donabedian framework evaluated interventions consisting of provider education, visual workflow cues, and audit-and-feedback. Pre-intervention data included 2,568 chart reviews from October 2025 to January 2026. Post-intervention data included 2,173 chart reviews from January to April 2026. Data were analyzed by comparing pre- and post-intervention adherence rates. Project limitations included manual chart extraction due to limited access to Epic reporting. Results Provider adherence to Rubeola IgG screening increased from 63.3% pre- intervention to 75.1% post-intervention, an 11.8% increase. Implications The implementation of provider education, visual cues, and audit-and-feedback showed that workflow-based interventions can improve adherence to evidence-based serology screening practices. These strategies identify nonimmune individuals, reduce community transmission during the surge, protect vulnerable populations, and avoid the operational costs of a facility outbreak.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the issue of inconsistent adherence to Rubeola IgG serology screening during prenatal visits, which poses health risks for nonimmune pregnant patients and their newborns.
Design and population
A pre-post quality improvement (QI) design was used, evaluating interventions at an outpatient women’s health clinic. Pre-intervention data included 2,568 chart reviews from October 2025 to January 2026, while post-intervention data included 2,173 chart reviews from January to April 2026.
Core methods
The study employed provider education, visual workflow cues, and audit-and-feedback as interventions guided by the Iowa Model and Donabedian framework. Data were analyzed by comparing pre- and post-intervention adherence rates.
Main results
Provider adherence to Rubeola IgG screening increased from 63.3% pre-intervention to 75.1% post-intervention, representing an 11.8% increase.
Interpretation and novelty
The implementation of workflow-based interventions demonstrated an effective approach to improving adherence to evidence-based serology screening practices for Rubeola.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted workflow interventions can enhance prenatal screening for Rubeola, thereby reducing the risk of transmission to nonimmune pregnant patients and their newborns.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by manual chart extraction due to restricted access to Epic reporting, which may introduce potential bias. The evidence strength is moderate.

利用地区级医院残留标本建立血清监测系统的可行性:以赞比亚乔马和恩多拉地区为例。

Feasibility of establishing a serosurveillance system using residual specimens from district-level hospitals: A case study in Choma and Ndola Districts of Zambia.
作者： Christine Prosperi, Natalya Kostandova, Andrea C. Carcelen, Kalumbu H. Matakala, Innocent C. Bwalya, Gloria Musukwa, Mutinta Hamahuwa, Irene Mutale, Kenny Situtu, Gershom Chongwe
摘要中文翻译与资源密集型的基于概率的调查相比,在医疗机构采集的残留血液样本提供了低成本且易于获得的样本来源,可通过血清学 监测 监测人群免疫力。尽管担心这些样本的代表性,但由于需要在 暴发 期间快速估计 血清流行率 以告知应对和预防,因此最近对使用残留样本的兴趣有所增加。尽管在某些情况下残留标本采集是 监测 系统的关键组成部分,但其在低收入和中等收入国家实施的证据有限。2021 年 9 月至 2022 年 7 月,我们在赞比亚两个地区的三个卫生机构开展了试点项目,以论证将残余血液样本采集纳入卫生系统以估算 麻疹 血清流行率 的可行性。通过这个试点项目,我们能够收集残留的儿童和成人样本,但如果要大规模实施残留样本收集,则需要进行一些修改,例如更好地融入实验室和卫生设施的日常运作。本文总结了我们设计和实施残血标本采集的经验,包括从医院采集残血标本的经验教训和建议。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了 暴发 期间对低成本、可快速部署的血清学 监测 的需求,强调了在低收入和中等收入国家实施基于残留样本的血清监测的证据差距。
设计与对象
2021 年 9 月至 2022 年 7 月期间,在赞比亚乔马区和恩多拉区的三个卫生机构开展了一个试点项目,以评估将残余血液样本采集纳入常规卫生系统以进行 麻疹 血清流行率 估算的可行性。
核心方法
该试点项目将残余血液样本采集整合到现有卫生系统工作流程中,以估计 麻疹 血清流行率,并确定了可能扩大规模的修改。
主要结果
该试点项目成功收集了残留的儿童和成人标本,但需要进行修改才能更广泛地实施,例如改进与实验室和卫生设施常规的整合。
解释与创新
该研究证明了在资源有限的环境中使用残留样本进行血清监测的可行性,解决了 监测 基础设施的关键差距,并为扩大规模提供了可行的经验教训。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持使用残留样本来生成快速的 血清流行率 估计,这可以为资源匮乏地区的 暴发 应对和预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
由于试点规模小且残留样本缺乏代表性,证据受到限制,需要谨慎解释并在更大规模的研究中进一步验证。

Feasibility of establishing a serosurveillance system using residual specimens from district-level hospitals: A case study in Choma and Ndola Districts of Zambia.

Authors: Christine Prosperi, Natalya Kostandova, Andrea C. Carcelen, Kalumbu H. Matakala, Innocent C. Bwalya, Gloria Musukwa, Mutinta Hamahuwa, Irene Mutale, Kenny Situtu, Gershom Chongwe
Original abstract (English)
Residual blood specimens collected at health care facilities provide a low-cost and readily available specimen source to monitor population immunity through serological surveillance compared to more resource-intensive probability-based surveys. Despite concerns about the representativeness of these specimens, there has recently been increased interest in the use of residual specimens, driven by the need for rapid estimates of seroprevalence during outbreaks to inform response and prevention. Although residual specimen collection is a key component of surveillance systems in some settings, there is limited evidence of its implementation in low- and middle-income countries. We conducted a pilot project at three health facilities in two districts in Zambia between September 2021 through July 2022 to demonstrate the feasibility of residual blood specimen collection integrated into the health system to estimate measles seroprevalence. Through this pilot project, we were able to collect residual pediatric and adult specimens but with some modifications needed if residual specimen collection were to be implemented at scale, such as better integration into the routine functioning of laboratories and health facilities. This paper summarizes our experiences designing and implementing this residual blood specimen collection, including the lessons learned and recommendations for collecting residual specimens from hospitals.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need for low-cost, rapidly deployable serological surveillance during outbreaks, highlighting a gap in evidence for implementing residual specimen-based serosurveillance in low- and middle-income countries.
Design and population
A pilot project was conducted at three health facilities in Choma and Ndola Districts, Zambia, between September 2021 and July 2022 to assess the feasibility of integrating residual blood specimen collection into routine health systems for measles seroprevalence estimation.
Core methods
The pilot integrated residual blood specimen collection into existing health system workflows to estimate measles seroprevalence, with modifications identified for potential scale-up.
Main results
The pilot successfully collected residual pediatric and adult specimens, though modifications were required for broader implementation, such as improved integration with laboratory and health facility routines.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrated the feasibility of using residual specimens for serosurveillance in resource-limited settings, addressing a critical gap in surveillance infrastructure and providing actionable lessons for scale-up.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the use of residual specimens to generate rapid seroprevalence estimates, which can inform outbreak response and prevention strategies in low-resource settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the small scale of the pilot and the lack of representativeness of residual specimens, warranting cautious interpretation and further validation in larger studies.

集成疾病绘图和灵活的扫描统计来识别和可视化空间集群。

Integrating disease mapping and flexible scan statistics to identify and visualize spatial clusters.
作者： Lina Wang, Haoqi Hu, Yaru Li, Danfei Zhang, Xiang Li, Zhengbin Zhang
摘要中文翻译准确描述疾病的空间分布对于了解病因和指导有针对性的公共卫生干预措施至关重要。本研究开发了一个整合疾病绘图和空间扫描统计的分析框架,以识别和验证疾病的空间聚类模式。该框架遵循“探索-验证-细化”工作流程,将定性可视化与定量空间分析相结合。等值线图直观地描绘了疾病的总体分布情况,为识别潜在的高风险区域提供了依据;灵活扫描统计(FleXScan)方法检测统计上显着的簇;点图通过控制人口密度进一步揭示最可能的集群内的内部异质性。本研究使用合成数据和真实的 麻疹 案例数据验证了该框架。结果表明,该框架大幅提高了聚类检测的准确性和可靠性,有助于本地高风险核心的定位,并为精准疾病控制提供了可操作的见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
结果表明,该框架大幅提高了聚类检测的准确性和可靠性,有助于本地高风险核心的定位,并为精准疾病控制提供了可操作的见解。
设计与对象
本研究开发了一个整合疾病绘图和空间扫描统计的分析框架,以识别和验证疾病的空间聚类模式。
核心方法
本研究使用合成数据和真实的 麻疹 案例数据验证了该框架。
主要结果
等值线图直观地描绘了疾病的总体分布情况,为识别潜在的高风险区域提供了依据;灵活扫描统计(FleXScan)方法检测统计上显着的簇;点图通过控制人口密度进一步揭示最可能的集群内的内部异质性。
解释与创新
该框架遵循“探索-验证-细化”工作流程,将定性可视化与定量空间分析相结合。
科研与公卫意义
准确描述疾病的空间分布对于了解病因和指导有针对性的公共卫生干预措施至关重要。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Integrating disease mapping and flexible scan statistics to identify and visualize spatial clusters.

Authors: Lina Wang, Haoqi Hu, Yaru Li, Danfei Zhang, Xiang Li, Zhengbin Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Accurately characterizing the spatial distribution of diseases is essential for understanding etiology and guiding targeted public health interventions. This study develops an analytical framework integrating disease mapping and spatial scan statistics to identify and validate the spatial clustering patterns of diseases. The framework follows an "exploration-validation-refinement" workflow, combining qualitative visualization with quantitative spatial analysis. Choropleth maps intuitively depict the overall distribution of diseases, providing a basis for identifying potential high-risk areas; the flexible scan statistics (FleXScan) method detects statistically significant clusters; and dot cartograms further reveal internal heterogeneity within the most likely clusters by controlling for population density. This study validated the framework using both synthetic data and real measles case data. The results indicate that the framework substantially improves the accuracy and reliability of cluster detection, facilitates the localization of local high-risk cores, and provides actionable insights for precision disease control.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The results indicate that the framework substantially improves the accuracy and reliability of cluster detection, facilitates the localization of local high-risk cores, and provides actionable insights for precision disease control.
Design and population
This study develops an analytical framework integrating disease mapping and spatial scan statistics to identify and validate the spatial clustering patterns of diseases.
Core methods
This study validated the framework using both synthetic data and real measles case data.
Main results
Choropleth maps intuitively depict the overall distribution of diseases, providing a basis for identifying potential high-risk areas; the flexible scan statistics (FleXScan) method detects statistically significant clusters; and dot cartograms further reveal internal heterogeneity within the most likely clusters by controlling for population density.
Interpretation and novelty
The framework follows an "exploration-validation-refinement" workflow, combining qualitative visualization with quantitative spatial analysis.
Scientific and public-health significance
Accurately characterizing the spatial distribution of diseases is essential for understanding etiology and guiding targeted public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on abstract only. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

现阶段世界和俄罗斯联邦流行性腮腺炎的流行病学特征、流行病学监测和预防

Epidemiological features of mumps, epidemiological surveillance and prevention in the world and in the Russian Federation at the present stage
作者： S. A. Rumbesht, A. Ya. Mindlina
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管腮腺炎多年来一直是 疫苗 可预防的感染,但它仍然是一个紧迫的公共卫生问题。该研究旨在解决了解流行性腮腺炎与 麻疹 和风疹相比的当前流行病学状况的需要。
设计与对象
对 2012 年至 2024 年期间世界范围内腮腺炎 发病率 的流行病学回顾性分析,与 麻疹 和风疹的 发病率 以及俄罗斯联邦 2007 年至 2024 年期间的 发病率 进行比较,同时考虑到腮腺炎 发病率 的分布和按年龄组划分的病例结构。
核心方法
使用置信区间评估差异的可靠性。样本取自世界卫生组织数据库和联邦统计观察表。
主要结果
The analysis showed that the incidence of mumps in the world and in the Russian Federation differs from the incidence of measles and rubella. The incidence of mumps, although at low numbers, tends to increase in Russia (the average annual growth rate is 7.8 %). The highest incidence rate is observed in the group of children aged 3–6 years (3.19 ± 0.22 per 100 thousand of the corresponding population group), while the lowest incidence rate is in the group of children under 1 year of age (0.5 ± 0.17 per 100 thousand of the corresponding population group).
解释与创新
流行性腮腺炎发病率呈上升趋势,3-6岁和7-14岁儿童的发病率较高,以及这些年龄组对病例结构的贡献最大,表明该感染的流行状况正在恶化。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,俄罗斯需要提高 疫苗接种 的覆盖率和及时性,以应对不断增加的流行性腮腺炎 发病率,特别是 3-6 岁儿童。
局限与证据强度
俄罗斯联邦预防性 疫苗接种 的覆盖率在疫情大流行的背景下有所下降,到 2024 年尚未达到大流行前的水平,但值得注意的是,覆盖率仅在 24 个月内达到。证据是中等的,因为它基于回顾性数据,不包括详细的统计分析。

Epidemiological features of mumps, epidemiological surveillance and prevention in the world and in the Russian Federation at the present stage

Authors: S. A. Rumbesht, A. Ya. Mindlina
Original abstract (English)
Despite the fact that mumps has been a vaccine-preventable infection for many years, it continues to be an urgent public health problem. Epidemiological surveillance and vaccine prophylaxis for mumps are carried out comprehensively together with measles and rubella. At the same time, mumps is given much less attention than measles and rubella. Aim : to assess the epidemic situation of mumps in the world and in the Russian Federation, the coverage of vaccination, revaccination and the timeliness of their implementation in Russia. To compare the incidence of mumps, measles and rubella. Materials and methods . A retrospective epidemiological analysis of the incidence of mumps in the world for the period 2012–2024 was carried out in comparison with the incidence of measles and rubella, as well as in the Russian Federation for the period 2007–2024, taking into account the distribution of the incidence of mumps and the structure of cases by age group. The coverage of preventive mumps vaccinations for the period 2012-2024 and the timeliness of vaccination for the period 2021–2024 in the Russian Federation are analyzed. The sample was taken from the World Health Organization database and from the federal statistical observation forms (No. The reliability of the differences was assessed using confidence intervals. The analysis showed that the incidence of mumps in the world and in the Russian Federation differs from the incidence of measles and rubella. In particular, the incidence increases do not always coincide, there are cyclical features and there are differences in the most epidemiologically significant age groups. The incidence of mumps in Russia, although at low numbers, tends to increase (the average annual growth rate is 7.8 %). The highest incidence rate is observed in the group of children aged 3–6 years (3.19 ± 0.22 per 100 thousand. of the corresponding population group), while the lowest incidence rate is in the group of children under 1 year of age (0.5 ± 0.17 per 100 thousand of the corresponding population group). It should be noted that the measles incidence rate is the highest in the group of children under 1 year of age. At the same time, the incidence of mumps among children under 1 year of age is growing at the highest rate of incidence (an increase rate of 4.6 %). The largest contribution to the structure of cases is made by groups of children aged 7–14 years and 3–6 years, the proportion of the total number of cases is 20.3 % and 19.9 %, respectively. The coverage of preventive vaccinations in the Russian Federation decreased against the background of the pandemic and by 2024 has not yet reached pre-pandemic values, while it should be noted that coverage is achieved only by 24 months. Revaccination coverage in 2023 and 2024 amounted to 71.27 % and 92.3 %, respectively. The presence of an upward trend in the incidence of mumps, high levels of morbidity in children aged 3–6 and 7–14 years, as well as the greatest contribution of these age groups to the structure of cases indicate a worsening epidemic situation for this infection. To a certain extent, the reasons for the deterioration of the epidemic situation are late vaccination and insufficient vaccination coverage. At the same time, differences in the incidence of mumps from the incidence of measles and rubella require consideration of changing approaches to epidemiological surveillance and prevention of mumps.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Despite the fact that mumps has been a vaccine-preventable infection for many years, it continues to be an urgent public health problem. The study aimed to address the need for understanding the current epidemiological situation of mumps in comparison to measles and rubella.
Design and population
A retrospective epidemiological analysis of the incidence of mumps in the world for the period 2012–2024 was carried out in comparison with the incidence of measles and rubella, as well as in the Russian Federation for the period 2007–2024, taking into account the distribution of the incidence of mumps and the structure of cases by age group.
Core methods
The reliability of the differences was assessed using confidence intervals. The sample was taken from the World Health Organization database and from the federal statistical observation forms.
Main results
The analysis showed that the incidence of mumps in the world and in the Russian Federation differs from the incidence of measles and rubella. The incidence of mumps, although at low numbers, tends to increase in Russia (the average annual growth rate is 7.8 %). The highest incidence rate is observed in the group of children aged 3–6 years (3.19 ± 0.22 per 100 thousand of the corresponding population group), while the lowest incidence rate is in the group of children under 1 year of age (0.5 ± 0.17 per 100 thousand of the corresponding population group).
Interpretation and novelty
The presence of an upward trend in the incidence of mumps, high levels of morbidity in children aged 3–6 and 7–14 years, as well as the greatest contribution of these age groups to the structure of cases indicate a worsening epidemic situation for this infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings indicate the need for improved vaccination coverage and timeliness in Russia to address the increasing incidence of mumps, particularly in children aged 3–6 years.
Limitations and evidence strength
The coverage of preventive vaccinations in the Russian Federation decreased against the background of the pandemic and by 2024 has not yet reached pre-pandemic values, while it should be noted that coverage is achieved only by 24 months. The evidence is moderate as it is based on retrospective data and does not include detailed statistical analyses.

灭活病毒培养物中尼帕病毒核衣壳抗原的快速检测。

A Rapid Detection of Nipah Virus Nucleocapsid Antigen in Inactivated Viral Cultures.
作者： Gyeongmu Choi, Jung Kim, Hansaem Lee, Pyeonghwa Jeon, Jae Hun Jeong, Yujin Jung, Jin Seo Park, Mi Kyoung Lee, Kyung-Chang Kim, Jong-Hwan Lee
摘要中文翻译尼帕病毒 (NiV) 是一种高度致命的人畜共患亨尼帕病毒,可引起严重的呼吸道疾病和脑炎,并且仍然是 暴发 防备的优先病原体。及时的 诊断 对于遏制至关重要,但在容易出现 暴发 且资源有限的环境中,基于核酸的检测通常不切实际。在这里,我们报告了基于纤维素纳米珠(CNB)增强的侧流免疫分析(LFIA)针对病毒核衣壳蛋白(NP)的NiV快速抗原测试的开发。通过迭代生物淘选从人类合成噬菌体展示文库(scFv 和 Fab)中分离出抗 NiV NP 抗体,将其重新格式化为全长 IgG,并使用 ELISA 和免疫荧光测定进行筛选。将四种先导抗体推进到夹心配对矩阵中,该矩阵确定了优化的捕获-检测配置(mAb #5 作为捕获;mAb #2 作为检测)。生物层干涉测量法证实了所选抗体的纳摩尔结合亲和力(mAb #5 的 KD = 39.50 nM;mAb #2 的 KD = 26.36 nM)。最终的 CNB-LFIA 在 15 分钟内产生结果,重组 NiV NP 的分析检测限达到 1 ng/mL。该检测未观察到与 SARS-CoV-2、SARS-CoV、MERS-CoV 或甲型流感病毒的交叉反应性,而仅在高抗原浓度 (2 μg/mL) 下观察到 麻疹病毒 NP 的弱交叉反应性。使用通过 RT-ddPCR 定量的 γ 辐射培养 NiV,LFIA 通过便携式阅读器检测到病毒载量降至 4.1 × 107 拷贝/mL(4.1 × 106 拷贝/反应),通过肉眼检测到 8.3 × 107 拷贝/mL(8.3 × 106 拷贝/反应)。总的来说,该 CNB-LFIA 提供快速、无需设备的 NiV 检测,支持其在资源有限的环境中床旁护理 诊断 的潜在实用性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尼帕病毒 (NiV) 是导致严重呼吸道疾病和脑炎的首要病原体,在无法进行核酸检测的资源有限地区,及时 诊断 对于 暴发 控制至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种针对 NiV 的快速抗原测试,使用针对病毒核衣壳蛋白的纤维素纳米珠增强侧流免疫测定法。
核心方法
从人类合成噬菌体展示文库中分离出抗 NiV 核衣壳蛋白抗体,将其重新格式化为全长 IgG,并通过 ELISA 和免疫荧光测定进行筛选,以确定最佳捕获-检测对。
主要结果
优化的检测方法检测到的重组 NiV 核衣壳蛋白低至 1 ng/mL,与 SARS-CoV-2、SARS-CoV、MERS-CoV 或甲型流感病毒没有交叉反应,并以 4.1 × 10^7 拷贝/mL 的读数器或 8.3 × 10^7 拷贝/mL 的肉眼检测到 γ 照射的培养 NiV。
解释与创新
作者将该测定解释为提供快速、无需设备的 NiV 检测,适合在 暴发 多发环境中进行即时护理,强调了其在分子诊断不可用的情况下的潜在用途。
科研与公卫意义
这种快速抗原测试可以在资源有限的环境中实现及时的 NiV 诊断,支持实验室基础设施有限的 暴发 遏制和患者管理。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告患者样本或现场表现的临床验证,限制了对培养病毒之外的诊断准确性和普遍性的评估。

A Rapid Detection of Nipah Virus Nucleocapsid Antigen in Inactivated Viral Cultures.

Authors: Gyeongmu Choi, Jung Kim, Hansaem Lee, Pyeonghwa Jeon, Jae Hun Jeong, Yujin Jung, Jin Seo Park, Mi Kyoung Lee, Kyung-Chang Kim, Jong-Hwan Lee
Original abstract (English)
Nipah virus (NiV) is a highly lethal zoonotic henipavirus that causes severe respiratory disease and encephalitis and remains a priority pathogen for outbreak preparedness. Timely diagnosis is essential for containment, yet nucleic acid-based testing is often impractical in outbreak-prone, resource-limited settings. Here, we report the development of a rapid antigen test for NiV based on a cellulose nanobead (CNB)-enhanced lateral flow immunoassay (LFIA) targeting the viral nucleocapsid protein (NP). Anti-NiV NP antibodies were isolated from human synthetic phage-display libraries (scFv and Fab) by iterative bio-panning, reformatted as full-length IgGs, and screened using ELISA and immunofluorescence assays. Four lead antibodies were advanced to a sandwich-pairing matrix, which identified an optimized capture-detection configuration (mAb #5 as capture; mAb #2 as detection). Bio-layer interferometry confirmed nanomolar binding affinities for the selected antibodies (KD = 39.50 nM for mAb #5; KD = 26.36 nM for mAb #2). The finalized CNB-LFIA produced results within 15 min and achieved an analytical limit of detection of 1 ng/mL for recombinant NiV NP. The assay observed no cross-reactivity with SARS-CoV-2, SARS-CoV, MERS-CoV, or influenza A, whereas weak cross-reactivity for measles virus NP was observed only at a high antigen concentration (2 μg/mL). Using γ-irradiated cultured NiV quantified by RT-ddPCR, the LFIA detected viral load down to 4.1 × 107 copies/mL (4.1 × 106 copies/reaction) by a portable reader and 8.3 × 107 copies/mL (8.3 × 106 copies/reaction) by the naked eye. Collectively, this CNB-LFIA provides rapid, equipment-free NiV detection, supporting its potential utility for point-of-care diagnosis in resource-limited settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Nipah virus (NiV) is a priority pathogen causing severe respiratory disease and encephalitis, and timely diagnosis is critical for outbreak control in resource-limited settings where nucleic acid-based testing is impractical.
Design and population
The study developed a rapid antigen test for NiV using a cellulose nanobead-enhanced lateral flow immunoassay targeting the viral nucleocapsid protein.
Core methods
Anti-NiV nucleocapsid protein antibodies were isolated from human synthetic phage-display libraries, reformatted as full-length IgGs, and screened by ELISA and immunofluorescence assays to identify optimal capture-detection pairs.
Main results
The optimized assay detected recombinant NiV nucleocapsid protein down to 1 ng/mL, showed no cross-reactivity with SARS-CoV-2, SARS-CoV, MERS-CoV, or influenza A, and detected γ-irradiated cultured NiV at 4.1 × 10^7 copies/mL by reader or 8.3 × 10^7 copies/mL by naked eye.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the assay as providing rapid, equipment-free NiV detection suitable for point-of-care use in outbreak-prone settings, highlighting its potential utility where molecular diagnostics are unavailable.
Scientific and public-health significance
This rapid antigen test could enable timely NiV diagnosis in resource-limited settings, supporting outbreak containment and patient management where laboratory infrastructure is limited.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report clinical validation in patient samples or field performance, limiting assessment of diagnostic accuracy and generalisability beyond cultured virus.

围产期妇女麻疹易感性筛查策略

A Measeles Susceptibility Screening Strategy for Perinatal Women
作者： Sandra N Davidson
摘要中文翻译目的:该质量改进项目旨在为德克萨斯州一家大型学术教学医院的围产期患者实施标准化的 麻疹 易感性筛查策略。该项目旨在通过将筛查整合到现有的产前工作流程中,提高提供者对常规 麻疹 免疫筛查和实验室订购的合规性。背景:麻疹 仍然是一种高度传染性疾病,德克萨斯州正在进行的 麻疹 显示出产前 麻疹 免疫筛查的差距。缺乏标准化筛查会增加错失识别非免疫患者并提供适当随访机会的风险。方法:使用计划-实施-研究-行动 (PDSA) 质量改进模型,在 10 周的时间内实施了提供者教育、电子病历 (EMR) 工作流程工具、标准化文档和每两周一次的绩效反馈。从 EMR 收集数据,并使用描述性统计数据和运行图表进行分析,以评估提供商对 麻疹 筛查和实验室订购的遵守情况。一些挑战包括延迟的信息技术支持和手动图表审查。结果:共有 475 名围产期患者接受了评估。358 名患者 (75%) 完成了 麻疹 免疫实验室筛查,而 117 名患者 (25%) 没有记录在案的筛查或实验室指令。在整个实施过程中,筛查合规性得到了改善。意义:通过将常规 麻疹 敏感性筛查整合到产前护理中,可以提高提供者的依从性,并促进早期识别非免疫患者。这种可持续的工作流程支持循证实践,加强疾病 暴发 准备,并为改善医疗机构中的孕产妇健康实践提供模型。

问题与背景
该质量改进项目旨在为德克萨斯州一家大型学术教学医院的围产期患者实施标准化的 麻疹 敏感性筛查策略。德克萨斯州正在进行的 麻疹 显示产前 麻疹 免疫筛查存在差距。

该项目旨在通过将筛查整合到现有的产前工作流程中,提高提供者对常规 麻疹 免疫筛查和实验室订购的合规性。总共对 475 名围产期患者进行了评估。

解释与创新
通过将常规 麻疹 敏感性筛查整合到产前护理中,提高了提供者的依从性并促进了非免疫患者的早期识别。这种可持续的工作流程支持循证实践,加强疾病 暴发 准备,并为改善医疗机构中的孕产妇健康实践提供模型。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为产前护理中标准化 麻疹 易感性筛查策略的制定提供信息,以提高提供者的依从性并识别非免疫患者。这有助于更好地做好疾病 暴发 准备和孕产妇保健实践。
局限与证据强度
一些挑战包括延迟的信息技术支持和手动图表审查。该证据是中等的,因为它是基于普遍性有限的质量改进项目。

A Measeles Susceptibility Screening Strategy for Perinatal Women

Authors: Sandra N Davidson
Original abstract (English)
Purpose: This quality improvement project aimed to implement a standardized measles susceptibility screening strategy for perinatal patients at a large academic teaching hospital in Texas. The project aimed to improve provider compliance with routine measles immunity screening and laboratory ordering by integrating screening into existing prenatal workflows. Background: Measles remains a highly contagious disease, and ongoing outbreaks in Texas showed gaps in prenatal measles immunity screening. The absence of standardized screening increases the risk of missed opportunities to identify non-immune patients and provide appropriate follow-up. Methodology: Using the Plan-Do-Study-Act (PDSA) quality improvement model, provider education, electronic medical record (EMR) workflow tools, standardized documentation, and biweekly performance feedback were implemented over a 10-week period. Data were collected from the EMR and analyzed using descriptive statistics and run charts to evaluate provider compliance with measles screening and laboratory ordering. Some challenges included delayed information technology support and manual chart reviews. Results: A total of 475 perinatal patients were evaluated. Measles immunity laboratory screening was completed for 358 patients (75%), while 117 patients (25%) had no documented screening or laboratory order. Screening compliance improved throughout implementation. Implications: By integrating routine measles susceptibility screening into prenatal care improved provider compliance and facilitated earlier identification of non-immune patients. This sustainable workflow supports evidence-based practice, strengthens disease outbreak preparedness, and provides a model for improving maternal health practices in healthcare settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This quality improvement project aimed to implement a standardized measles susceptibility screening strategy for perinatal patients at a large academic teaching hospital in Texas. Ongoing outbreaks in Texas showed gaps in prenatal measles immunity screening.
Design and population
The project aimed to improve provider compliance with routine measles immunity screening and laboratory ordering by integrating screening into existing prenatal workflows. A total of 475 perinatal patients were evaluated.
Core methods
Using the Plan-Do-Study-Act (PDSA) quality improvement model, provider education, electronic medical record (EMR) workflow tools, standardized documentation, and biweekly performance feedback were implemented over a 10-week period. Data were collected from the EMR and analyzed using descriptive statistics and run charts to evaluate provider compliance with measles screening and laboratory ordering.
Main results
Measles immunity laboratory screening was completed for 358 patients (75%), while 117 patients (25%) had no documented screening or laboratory order. Screening compliance improved throughout implementation.
Interpretation and novelty
By integrating routine measles susceptibility screening into prenatal care, improved provider compliance and facilitated earlier identification of non-immune patients were achieved. This sustainable workflow supports evidence-based practice, strengthens disease outbreak preparedness, and provides a model for improving maternal health practices in healthcare settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of standardized measles susceptibility screening strategies in prenatal care to improve provider compliance and identify non-immune patients. This can contribute to better disease outbreak preparedness and maternal health practices.
Limitations and evidence strength
Some challenges included delayed information technology support and manual chart reviews. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a quality improvement project with limited generalizability.
